🧪 Model v16 upgrades (Oct 17)

Walk-forward / held-out tests only; CFB uses 2022-25 only. A part goes live only if it beat the current model on data it never saw.

1 · Outcome distributions + alt-line pricingBET
LIVE for NFL · CFB beyond ±3 pts only
Bug fixedfake +30% alt edges (wrong game / same-sign devig) gone
NFL margin, held-out 2023-25exact log-lik -3.8298 → -3.8227 (alt-line log-loss 0.55584 → 0.55583, ≈ same)
NFL totals, held-out 2023-25alt log-loss 0.57506 → 0.56927 (better)
Pinnacle ladder check, NFL spreadserror 2.6 → 1.0 pp
Pinnacle ladder check, CFB spreadserror 1.7 → 2.1 pp (worse → CFB keeps the old rule within 3 pts)
Prices any alt spread/total/ML from Pinnacle's main line, with push odds (3 = 7.6% predicted vs 7.6% actual NFL). 2026 NFL results (79 games) slightly favoured the old rule within ±3: small sample, watching.
2 · Market-anchored CLV model (CFB)LEAN / CAUTION
INFO ONLY: does not beat the current v2 rule
2024 top-15% picksCLV +0.34 pts, line moved our way 60%
2025 top-15% picksCLV +1.82 pts, 76%
Current v2 rule (same years)+0.37 / +1.87 pts CLV
Raw model edge ranking+0.06 / +0.73 pts
Predicts opener→close movement; beats ranking by raw model edge but only ties the existing rule, so it's shown as 'predicted CLV' on college cards, not used to pick or stake. NFL: no free historical openers yet.
3 · CFB ratings r3 (opponent-adjusted, recency, Bayesian prior)LEAN / CAUTION
FLAGGED: not live (didn't beat v2 vs the close)
2024 v2 → r3MAE 12.872 · ATS≥3 49.3% (n=343) · log-loss 0.6941 → MAE 12.814 · ATS≥3 49.6% (n=375) · log-loss 0.6939
2025 v2 → r3MAE 12.349 · ATS≥3 52.9% (n=331) · log-loss 0.6928 → MAE 12.361 · ATS≥3 50.5% (n=386) · log-loss 0.6931
r3+v2 blendMAE 12.76 / 12.278 (better both years) but log-loss vs close not better
Closing line MAE12.136 / 11.824 (still the best predictor)
Prior strength κ=6.0, half-life 4 weeks, tuned on 2023 only. Shown on college cards as a second opinion.
4 · Player prop engine v2 (NFL)BET
LIVE: receptions, rec yds, rush yds, anytime TD
rec ydslog-loss 0.6586 → 0.643 · Brier 0.2328 → 0.2259
receptionslog-loss 0.6501 → 0.6353 · Brier 0.2297 → 0.2226
rush ydslog-loss 0.6668 → 0.6498 · Brier 0.2367 → 0.2294
anytime tdlog-loss 0.5171 → 0.5105 · Brier 0.1694 → 0.167
Real FD lines so far1 game (SEA@DEN, 43 props): v2 0.61 vs market 0.71 log-loss — far too small to trust
Usage shares (targets, carries, snaps, recency-weighted), defence vs position, game script from spread/total, negative-binomial / gamma distributions, injured players' volume re-allocated. Still shrunk 50% toward the market. Passing yards unchanged.
